> 177	                      : { imageUrl: lastSuccess.output_url }),
> 178	                    generatedAt: lastSuccess.created_at,
> 179	                  },
> 180	                }
> 181	              : {}),
> 182	            error: undefined,
> 183	          },
> 184	        };
> 185	      }
> 186	      return node;
> 187	    });
> 188	
> 189	    initialize(project.id, {
> 190	      nodes: reconciledNodes,
> 191	      edges: graph.edges ?? [],
> 192	      pipelineMode: graph.pipelineMode,
> 193	      lanes: graph.lanes,
> 194	      styleSettings: graph.styleSettings,
> 195	      viewport: project.viewport,
> 196	    });
> 197	    const t = setTimeout(() => useCanvasStore.temporal.getState().clear(), 600);
> 198	    return () => clearTimeout(t);
> 199	  }, [project, executions, initialize, currentProjectId]);
> 200	
> 201	  if (isLoading) {
> 202	    return (
> 203	      <div className="flex h-full items-center justify-center">
> 204	        <Loader2 className="h-8 w-8 animate-spin text-primary" />
> 205	      </div>
> 206	    );
> 207	  }
> 208	
> 209	  return (
> 210	    <>
> 211	      <Helmet>
> 212	        <title>{project?.name || "Canvas"} — Canvas Studio — MCORCH</title>
> 213	      </Helmet>
> 214	      <div className="flex h-full w-full flex-col">
> 215	        <ReactFlowProvider>
> 216	          <CanvasShell projectId={projectId} project={project ?? null} />
> 217	        </ReactFlowProvider>
> 218	      </div>
> 219	    </>
> 220	  );
> 221	}
> 222	
> 223	function CanvasShell({
> 224	  projectId,
> 225	  project,
> 226	}: {
> 227	  projectId: string | undefined;
> 228	  project: CanvasProject | null;
> 229	}) {
> 230	  useReactFlow(); // bind provider — instance used by AddNodeMenu via context
> 231	
> 232	  const nodes = useCanvasStore((s) => s.nodes);
> 233	  const edges = useCanvasStore((s) => s.edges);
> 234	  const pipelineMode = useCanvasStore((s) => s.pipelineMode);
> 235	  const onNodesChange = useCanvasStore((s) => s.onNodesChange);
> 236	  const onEdgesChange = useCanvasStore((s) => s.onEdgesChange);
> 237	  const onConnect = useCanvasStore((s) => s.onConnect);
> 238	  const setViewport = useCanvasStore((s) => s.setViewport);
> 239	  const selectNode = useCanvasStore((s) => s.selectNode);
> 240	  const updateNodeData = useCanvasStore((s) => s.updateNodeData);
> 241	
> 242	  const autoSave = useAutoSave(projectId ?? null);
> 243	  const { profile, refetch: refetchDashboard } = useDashboardData();
> 244	  const executeNode = useCanvasExecute();
> 245	  const updateProject = useUpdateCanvasProject();
> 246	
> 247	  const onNodeClick = useCallback(
> 248	    (_: unknown, node: Node) => {
> 249	      selectNode(node.id);
> 250	    },
> 251	    [selectNode],
> 252	  );
> 253	
> 254	  const {
> 255	    pipelineStatus,
> 256	    runPipeline,
> 257	    runnableCount,
> 258	    runAllCost,
> 259	  } = useCanvasPipeline({ projectId, refetchDashboard });
> 260	
> 261	  // Phase 4.2c — Realtime bridge: webhook flips vm_canvas_executions.status,
> 262	  // we reconcile the Zustand store so the canvas node UI updates without refresh.
> 263	  useCanvasJobsRealtime(projectId, (row) => {
> 264	    if (!row.node_id) return;
> 265	    if (row.status === "success") {
> 266	      updateNodeData(row.node_id, {
> 267	        status: "success",
> 268	        output: {
> 269	          videoUrl: row.output_url ?? undefined,
> 270	          imageUrl: row.output_url ?? undefined,
> 271	          generatedAt: row.completed_at ?? new Date().toISOString(),
> 272	        },
> 273	        error: undefined,
> 274	      });
> 275	      refetchDashboard?.();
> 276	    } else if (
> 277	      row.status === "failed" ||
> 278	      row.status === "timeout" ||
> 279	      row.status === "cancelled"
> 280	    ) {
> 281	      updateNodeData(row.node_id, {
> 282	        status: "failed",
> 283	        error: row.error_message ?? row.status,
> 284	      });
> 285	    } else if (row.status === "queued" || row.status === "running") {
> 286	      updateNodeData(row.node_id, { status: "running" });
> 287	    }
> 288	  });
> 289	
> 290	  return (
> 291	    <>
> 292	      <TopBar
> 293	        name={project?.name ?? "Sem título"}
> 294	        onRename={(name) => {
> 295	          if (projectId) updateProject.mutate({ projectId, name });
> 296	        }}
> 297	        saving={autoSave.status === "saving"}
> 298	        onSaveNow={() => autoSave.saveNow()}
> 299	        onRunAll={runPipeline}
> 300	        pipelineStatus={pipelineStatus}
> 301	        runnableCount={runnableCount}
> 302	        runAllCost={runAllCost}
> 303	        runAllDisabled={!projectId}
> 304	      />
> 305	
> 306	      <div className="flex min-h-0 flex-1">
> 307	        <LeftPanel projectId={projectId} />
> 308	
> 309	        <div className="relative flex-1 min-w-0">
> 310	          <ReactFlow
> 311	            nodes={nodes}
> 312	            edges={edges}
> 313	            onNodesChange={onNodesChange}
> 314	            onEdgesChange={onEdgesChange}
> 315	            onConnect={onConnect}
> 316	            onNodeClick={onNodeClick}
> 317	            onPaneClick={() => selectNode(null)}
> 318	            onMoveEnd={(_, vp) => setViewport(vp)}
> 319	            nodeTypes={nodeTypes}
> 320	            edgeTypes={edgeTypes}
> 321	            defaultViewport={project?.viewport ?? { x: 0, y: 0, zoom: 1 }}
> 322	            snapToGrid={pipelineMode}
> 323	            snapGrid={[COL_WIDTH, LANE_HEIGHT]}
> 324	            deleteKeyCode={["Backspace", "Delete"]}
> 325	            fitView
> 326	            className="bg-background"
> 327	          >
> 328	            {pipelineMode && <LanesOverlay />}
> 329	            <Background gap={20} size={1} color="rgba(255,255,255,0.03)" />
> 330	            <Controls className="!border-border/50 !bg-card/80" />
> 331	            <MiniMap
> 332	              className="!border-border/50 !bg-card/80"
> 333	              nodeColor={() => "hsl(var(--primary))"}
> 334	              maskColor="rgba(0,0,0,0.5)"
> 335	            />
> 336	          </ReactFlow>
> 337	
> 338	          {/* Add-node FAB (bottom-right) + Cmd+K palette (global) */}
> 339	          <AddNodeFab />
> 340	          <AddNodeCommand />
> 341	        </div>
> 342	
> 343	        {/* Typed inspectors panel — routes by node.data.kind */}
> 344	        <RightPanel
> 345	          projectId={projectId ?? ""}
> 346	          creditsBalance={profile?.mco_balance ?? 0}
> 347	          onCreditsChanged={() => refetchDashboard?.()}
> 348	        />
> 349	      </div>
> 350	
> 351	      <StatusBar
> 352	        saveStatus={autoSave.status}
> 353	        lastSaved={autoSave.lastSaved}
> 354	        creditsBalance={profile?.mco_balance ?? 0}
> 355	        projectExecutions={project?.total_executions ?? 0}
> 356	      />
> 357	    </>
> 358	  );
> 359	}
> 360	
> ```

> 50	 * studio catalog. Higgsfield Soul entries encode resolution × batch in
> 51	 * the suffix; canvas-execute resolves these to the canonical tier key
> 52	 * `higgsfield/soul-<res>-<batch>` (see CREDIT_COSTS in canvas-execute).
> 53	 */
> 54	export type ImageModelId =
> 55	  // Higgsfield Soul (text-to-image)
> 56	  | "soul-720p-x1"
> 57	  | "soul-720p-x4"
> 58	  | "soul-1080p-x1"
> 59	  | "soul-1080p-x4"
> 60	  // OpenAI
> 61	  | "dall-e-3"
> 62	  | "gpt-image-1"
> 63	  // Replicate
> 64	  | "flux-1.1-pro"
> 65	  | "flux-schnell"
> 66	  | "sdxl"
> 67	  // OpenRouter (chat-completions multimodal — Round 3 hotfix 2026-05-17)
> 68	  | "openrouter-gemini-image"
> 69	  | "openrouter-flux-pro"
> 70	  // Legacy — kept in union for saved-state compat; UI banner steers users to the new entries.
> 71	  | "openrouter-dall-e-3";
> 72	
> 73	export interface ImageModelInfo {
> 74	  label: string;
> 75	  provider: ProviderId;
> 76	  /** Model name passed to canvas-execute (`body.model`). */
> 77	  modelParam: string;
> 78	  tier: "Premium" | "Padrão" | "Rápido" | "Validação";
> 79	  /** mcoCoins debitados na conta MCORCH. */
> 80	  coinCost: number;
> 81	  /** Créditos Higgsfield reais consumidos da conta paga (se aplicável). */
> 82	  higgsfieldCost?: number;
> 83	  /** ETA estimado para a geração. */
> 84	  eta: string;
> 85	  /** Soul-only: resolução nativa (sent via parameters.resolution). */
> 86	  resolution?: "720p" | "1080p";
> 87	  /** Soul-only: batch size (sent via parameters.batch). */
> 88	  batch?: 1 | 4;
> 89	}

> 286	/* ── Safe catalog lookups (NEVER white-screen on an unknown modelId) ──────
> 287	 * A project can be saved/recovered with a modelId that isn't a catalog key —
> 288	 * e.g. a graph rebuilt from vm_canvas_executions stores the RAW execution model
> 289	 * ("google/gemini-2.5-flash-image", "dop-standard") instead of the UI key
> 290	 * ("openrouter-gemini-image", "dop-standard-5s"). Direct `MODEL_INFO[id].coinCost`
> 291	 * then throws "Cannot read properties of undefined", which crashed the ENTIRE
> 292	 * editor (white page + only the footer). These accessors degrade gracefully so a
> 293	 * stale/foreign modelId renders a neutral node instead of taking down the page. */
> 294	const FALLBACK_IMAGE_MODEL_INFO: ImageModelInfo = {
> 295	  label: "Modelo fora do catálogo",
> 296	  provider: "openrouter",
> 297	  modelParam: "",
> 298	  tier: "Padrão",
> 299	  coinCost: 0,
> 300	  eta: "—",
> 301	};
> 302	const FALLBACK_VIDEO_MODEL_INFO: VideoModelInfo = {
> 303	  label: "Modelo de vídeo fora do catálogo",
> 304	  provider: "higgsfield",
> 305	  modelParam: "",
> 306	  tier: "Padrão",
> 307	  coinCost: 0,
> 308	  eta: "—",
> 309	};
> 310	
> 311	export function getImageModelInfo(id: string | null | undefined): ImageModelInfo {
> 312	  return (id != null && MODEL_INFO[id as ImageModelId]) || FALLBACK_IMAGE_MODEL_INFO;
> 313	}
> 314	export function getVideoModelInfo(id: string | null | undefined): VideoModelInfo {
> 315	  return (id != null && VIDEO_MODEL_INFO[id as VideoModelId]) || FALLBACK_VIDEO_MODEL_INFO;
> 316	}

> 460	/**
> 461	 * avatar-clone-ai Fatia 2 — voiceClone node (FR-AC-008/009/010/011).
> 462	 * Clones a reference voice (ElevenLabs IVC / Cartesia) then synthesizes a script with it.
> 463	 * Runs against the net-new `generate-voice` Edge Function (NOT canvas-execute).
> 464	 */
> 465	export interface VoiceCloneData {
> 466	  kind: "voiceClone";
> 467	  label: string;
> 468	  /** Provider for both the clone and the synthesis (BYOK per-user). */
> 469	  provider: "elevenlabs" | "cartesia";
> 470	  /** Single language per generation — never code-switch intra-sentence (FM-AC-013). */
> 471	  language: "en" | "pt-BR";
> 472	  /** Uploaded reference-audio sample used to clone the voice. */
> 473	  sampleAudioUrl?: string;
> 474	  sampleStoragePath?: string;
> 475	  /** voice_profiles row id — set after a successful clone; required to synthesize. */
> 476	  voiceProfileId?: string;
> 477	  /** Text to synthesize with the cloned voice. */
> 478	  prompt: string;
> 479	  status: NodeStatus;
> 480	  output?: {
> 481	    audioUrl?: string;
> 482	    creativeAssetId?: string;
> 483	    generatedAt: string;
> 484	  };
> 485	  error?: string;
> 486	}
> 487	
> 488	/**
> 489	 * avatar-clone-ai Fatia 3b — avatarIdentity node (FR-AC-012).
> 490	 * Holds a consented reference-portrait set + the trained identity profile. Trains against the
> 491	 * net-new `avatar-identity-train` Edge Function (gated OTD-AC-017) — NOT canvas-execute. The
> 492	 * Identity Gate runs the VLM-soft compare (FR-AC-013b, src/lib/avatar-identity.ts) post-generation.
> 493	 */
> 494	export interface AvatarIdentityData {
> 495	  kind: "avatarIdentity";
> 496	  label: string;
> 497	  /** self_clone (titular cloning themselves) drives the FR-AC-032 verification + compliance template. */
> 498	  personaType: "self_clone" | "fictional";
> 499	  /** Identity ladder tier (1 InstantID/IP-Adapter · 2 PuLID-Flux · 3 LoRA per-person). */
> 500	  tier: 1 | 2 | 3;
> 501	  provider: "higgsfield" | "lora_flux";
> 502	  /** Identity Gate mode — VLM-soft MVP default (FR-AC-013/014); arcface deferred OTD-AC-015. */
> 503	  gateMode: "vlm_soft" | "arcface";
> 504	  /** Uploaded reference-portrait signed URLs (≥20 recommended for Soul ID). */
> 505	  referenceUrls: string[];
> 506	  referenceStoragePaths?: string[];
> 507	  /** avatar_identities row id — set after a successful train (gated OTD-AC-017). */
> 508	  avatarIdentityId?: string;
> 509	  status: NodeStatus;
> 510	  output?: {
> 511	    /** last generated frame URL that ran through the Identity Gate */
> 512	    frameUrl?: string;
> 513	    softMatch?: "pass" | "review" | "fail";
> 514	    generatedAt: string;
> 515	  };
> 516	  error?: string;
> 517	}
> 518	
> 519	export type CanvasNodeData =
> 520	  | GenerateImageData
> 521	  | CharacterReferenceData
> 522	  | SceneComposeData
> 523	  | StyleTransferData
> 524	  | ImageToVideoData
> 525	  | VoiceCloneData
> 526	  | AvatarIdentityData;
> 527	
> 528	export type CanvasNode = Node<CanvasNodeData, CanvasNodeKind>;
> 529	export type CanvasEdge = Edge;
> 530	
> 531	export interface CanvasGraph {
> 532	  nodes: CanvasNode[];
> 533	  edges: CanvasEdge[];
> 534	}
> 535	
> 536	export const EMPTY_GRAPH: CanvasGraph = { nodes: [], edges: [] };
> 537	
> 538	/* ── Pipeline mode (Sprint 4.1 — preserved) ──────────────────────────── */
> 539	
> 540	export const LANE_HEIGHT = 320;
> 541	export const COL_WIDTH = 320;
> 542	export const MIN_LANES = 4;
> 543	
> 544	export interface LaneConfig {
> 545	  id: string;
> 546	  label: string;
> 547	}
> 548	
> 549	export function makeLane(label = "Nova lane"): LaneConfig {
> 550	  return { id: `lane_${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 10)}`, label };
> 551	}
> 552	
> 553	export function laneIndexFromY(y: number): number {
> 554	  return Math.max(0, Math.floor(y / LANE_HEIGHT));
> 555	}

> 581	/* ── Persisted graph blob (Sprint 4.1 — preserved) ───────────────────── */
> 582	
> 583	export interface PersistedCanvasGraph extends CanvasGraph {
> 584	  pipelineMode?: boolean;
> 585	  lanes?: LaneConfig[];
> 586	  styleSettings?: StyleSettings;

> 590	  viewport?: Viewport;
> 591	}

> 593	/* ── Factories ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */
> 594	
> 595	function makeNodeId(): string {
> 596	  return `n_${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 10)}`;
> 597	}
> 598	
> 599	export function makeDefaultGenerateImage(
> 600	  position: { x: number; y: number },
> 601	  overrides: Partial<GenerateImageData> = {},
> 602	): CanvasNode {
> 603	  return {
> 604	    id: makeNodeId(),
> 605	    type: "generateImage",
> 606	    position,
> 607	    data: {
> 608	      kind: "generateImage",
> 609	      label: "Gerar Imagem",
> 610	      modelId: DEFAULT_IMAGE_MODEL,
> 611	      prompt: "",
> 612	      negativePrompt: "",
> 613	      aspectRatio: "9:16",
> 614	      controls: { ...DEFAULT_CONTROLS },
> 615	      seed: null,
> 616	      fixedSeed: false,

> 751	/* ── Canvas-execute payload helper ───────────────────────────────────── */
> 752	
> 753	/**
> 754	 * Helper to compile prompt incorporating project level StyleSettings
> 755	 */
> 756	export function compileFinalPrompt(
> 757	  prompt: string,
> 758	  styleSettings?: StyleSettings,
> 759	  inheritStyle?: boolean,
> 760	): string {
> 761	  if (!inheritStyle || !styleSettings) return prompt;
> 762	
> 763	  const parts: string[] = [];
> 764	  if (prompt.trim()) {
> 765	    parts.push(prompt.trim());
> 766	  }
> 767	
> 768	  const { stylePreset, mood, palette } = styleSettings;
> 769	
> 770	  if (mood && mood.trim()) {
> 771	    parts.push(`mood: ${mood.trim()}`);
> 772	  }
> 773	
> 774	  if (stylePreset && stylePreset !== "none") {
> 775	    const presetSuffixes: Record<StylePresetId, string> = {
> 776	      none: "",
> 777	      cinematic: "cinematic film still, anamorphic lens, dramatic lighting, 35mm film grain, shallow depth of field",
> 778	      photorealistic: "highly detailed photorealistic image, 8k resolution, natural lighting, high dynamic range",
> 779	      anime: "anime illustration style, cel shaded, vibrant colors, expressive line art",
> 780	      illustration: "artistic illustration, hand-drawn texture, stylized composition",
> 781	      noir: "film noir aesthetic, high contrast black and white, dramatic shadows, moody lighting",
> 782	      documentary: "documentary style photography, candid capture, natural lighting, realistic grain",
> 783	    };
> 784	    const suffix = presetSuffixes[stylePreset];
> 785	    if (suffix) {
> 786	      parts.push(suffix);
> 787	    }
> 788	  }
> 789	
> 790	  if (palette && palette.length > 0) {
> 791	    const validColors = palette.filter(c => c && c.trim());
> 792	    if (validColors.length > 0) {
> 793	      parts.push(`color palette: ${validColors.join(", ")}`);
> 794	    }
> 795	  }
> 796	
> 797	  return parts.join(", ");
> 798	}
> 799	
> 800	/**
> 801	 * Translate a typed node's data into the request body accepted by the
> 802	 * `canvas-execute` Edge Function. Soul models split into provider/model
> 803	 * plus parameters.resolution/batch so the function's tiering helper
> 804	 * (`resolveHiggsfieldSoulKey`) computes the correct cost.
> 805	 */
> 806	export function resolveExecutePayload(
> 807	  data: CanvasNodeData,
> 808	  styleSettings?: StyleSettings,
> 809	): {
> 810	  node_type: string;
> 811	  provider: ProviderId;
> 812	  model: string;
> 813	  prompt: string;
> 814	  negative_prompt?: string;
> 815	  parameters: Record<string, unknown>;
> 816	} | null {
> 817	  switch (data.kind) {
> 818	    case "generateImage": {
> 819	      const info = getImageModelInfo(data.modelId);
> 820	      const parameters: Record<string, unknown> = {
> 821	        aspect_ratio: data.aspectRatio,
> 822	        controls: data.controls,
> 823	        seed: data.fixedSeed && data.seed != null ? data.seed : undefined,
> 824	      };
> 825	      if (info.resolution) parameters.resolution = info.resolution;
> 826	      if (info.batch) parameters.batch = info.batch;
> 827	      return {
> 828	        node_type: "generate_image",
> 829	        provider: info.provider,
> 830	        model: info.modelParam,
> 831	        prompt: compileFinalPrompt(data.prompt, styleSettings, data.inheritStyle),
> 832	        negative_prompt: data.negativePrompt || undefined,
> 833	        parameters,
> 834	      };
> 835	    }
> 836	    case "sceneCompose": {
> 837	      const info = getImageModelInfo(data.modelId);
> 838	      const parameters: Record<string, unknown> = {
> 839	        aspect_ratio: data.aspectRatio,
> 840	      };
> 841	      if (info.resolution) parameters.resolution = info.resolution;
> 842	      if (info.batch) parameters.batch = info.batch;
> 843	      return {
> 844	        node_type: "scene_compose",
> 845	        provider: info.provider,
> 846	        model: info.modelParam,
> 847	        prompt: compileFinalPrompt(data.prompt, styleSettings, data.inheritStyle),
> 848	        parameters,
> 849	      };
> 850	    }
> 851	    case "styleTransfer": {
> 852	      const preset = STYLE_TRANSFER_PRESETS.find((p) => p.id === data.presetId);
> 853	      return {
> 854	        node_type: "style_transfer",
> 855	        provider: "higgsfield",
> 856	        model: "soul",
> 857	        prompt: preset?.suffix ?? "",
> 858	        parameters: {
> 859	          strength: data.strength,
> 860	          preset_id: data.presetId,
> 861	          resolution: "720p",
> 862	          batch: 1,
> 863	        },
> 864	      };
> 865	    }
> 866	    case "imageToVideo": {
> 867	      const info = getVideoModelInfo(data.videoModel);
> 868	      return {
> 869	        node_type: "image_to_video",
> 870	        provider: info.provider,
> 871	        model: info.modelParam,
> 872	        prompt: data.prompt,
> 873	        parameters: {
> 874	          duration: data.duration,
> 875	          aspect_ratio: data.aspectRatio,
> 876	          motion_strength: data.motionStrength,
> 877	          async_only: info.asyncOnly ?? false,
> 878	        },
> 879	      };
> 880	    }
> 881	    case "characterReference":
> 882	      // CharacterReference doesn't trigger canvas-execute by itself —
> 883	      // it's a data source consumed by downstream sceneCompose/styleTransfer.
> 884	      return null;
> 885	    case "voiceClone":
> 886	      // voiceClone runs against the net-new `generate-voice` Edge Function (FR-AC-009),
> 887	      // NOT canvas-execute — its inspector calls useAvatarVoice directly.
> 888	      return null;
> 889	    case "avatarIdentity":
> 890	      // avatarIdentity trains against the net-new `avatar-identity-train` Edge Function
> 891	      // (FR-AC-012, gated OTD-AC-017), NOT canvas-execute — inspector handles it directly.
> 892	      return null;
> 893	  }
> 894	}
> 895	
> 896	/**
> 897	 * Type-guard helpers (used by node components and inspectors to narrow
> 898	 * the union via switch/case on `kind`).
> 899	 */
> 900	export function isGenerateImage(data: CanvasNodeData): data is GenerateImageData {
> 901	  return data.kind === "generateImage";
> 902	}
> 903	export function isCharacterReference(data: CanvasNodeData): data is CharacterReferenceData {
> 904	  return data.kind === "characterReference";
> 905	}
> 906	export function isSceneCompose(data: CanvasNodeData): data is SceneComposeData {
> 907	  return data.kind === "sceneCompose";
> 908	}
> 909	export function isStyleTransfer(data: CanvasNodeData): data is StyleTransferData {
> 910	  return data.kind === "styleTransfer";
> 911	}
> 912	export function isImageToVideo(data: CanvasNodeData): data is ImageToVideoData {
> 913	  return data.kind === "imageToVideo";
> 914	}
> 915	export function isVoiceClone(data: CanvasNodeData): data is VoiceCloneData {
> 916	  return data.kind === "voiceClone";
> 917	}
> 918	export function isAvatarIdentity(data: CanvasNodeData): data is AvatarIdentityData {
> 919	  return data.kind === "avatarIdentity";
> 920	}
> 921	
> 922	/** Resolve the mcoCoins cost of a single node from MODEL_INFO/VIDEO_MODEL_INFO. */
> 923	export function estimateNodeCost(data: CanvasNodeData): number {
> 924	  switch (data.kind) {
> 925	    case "generateImage":
> 926	      return getImageModelInfo(data.modelId).coinCost;
> 927	    case "sceneCompose":
> 928	      return data.cost;
> 929	    case "styleTransfer":
> 930	      return data.cost;
> 931	    case "imageToVideo":
> 932	      return getVideoModelInfo(data.videoModel).coinCost;
> 933	    case "characterReference":
> 934	      return 0;
> 935	    case "voiceClone":
> 936	      return 36; // COIN_COSTS.VOICE_GENERATION (charged on synthesize; clone is free)
> 937	    case "avatarIdentity":
> 938	      return 0; // train (FR-AC-012) is gated OTD-AC-017; cost charged server-side when wired (OTD-AC-006)
> 939	  }
> 940	}
> 941	
> ```

> 151	export function useSaveCanvasGraph() {
> 152	  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
> 153	
> 154	  return useMutation({
> 155	    mutationFn: async ({ projectId, graph, viewport }: {
> 156	      projectId: string;
> 157	      graph: PersistedCanvasGraph;
> 158	      viewport?: { x: number; y: number; zoom: number };
> 159	    }) => {
> 160	      // Hydration-race guard (data-loss fix): NEVER overwrite a non-empty saved graph with an empty one.
> 161	      // useAutoSave can flush (debounce / pagehide / unmount) BEFORE initialize() hydrates the store, so the
> 162	      // empty boot state {nodes:[]} would clobber a rich DB row → all canvas nodes lost (assets survive in
> 163	      // vm_canvas_executions, which is why the project still shows assets but no nodes). When the incoming
> 164	      // graph is empty, read-before-write and skip the clobber (viewport-only updates still persist).
> 165	      if (graph.nodes.length === 0) {
> 166	        const { data: existing } = await supabase
> 167	          .from("vm_canvas_projects")
> 168	          .select("graph")
> 169	          .eq("id", projectId)
> 170	          .maybeSingle();
> 171	        const existingNodes = (existing?.graph as PersistedCanvasGraph | null)?.nodes ?? [];
> 172	        if (existingNodes.length > 0) {
> 173	          console.warn(
> 174	            `[saveCanvasGraph] blocked empty-graph overwrite of ${existingNodes.length}-node project ${projectId} (hydration race)`,
> 175	          );
> 176	          if (viewport) {
> 177	            await supabase.from("vm_canvas_projects").update({ viewport }).eq("id", projectId);
> 178	          }
> 179	          return;
> 180	        }
> 181	      }
> 182	
> 183	      const updateData: any = { graph };
> 184	      if (viewport) updateData.viewport = viewport;
> 185	
> 186	      const { error } = await supabase
> 187	        .from("vm_canvas_projects")
> 188	        .update(updateData)
> 189	        .eq("id", projectId);
> 190	      if (error) throw error;
> 191	    },
> 192	    onSuccess: (_, vars) => {
> 193	      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["vm-canvas-project", vars.projectId] });
> 194	    },
> 195	  });
> 196	}
> 197

> 217	export function useCanvasExecute() {
> 218	  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
> 219	
> 220	  return useMutation({
> 221	    mutationFn: async (params: {
> 222	      project_id: string;
> 223	      node_id: string;
> 224	      node_type: string;
> 225	      provider: string;
> 226	      model: string;
> 227	      prompt: string;
> 228	      negative_prompt?: string;
> 229	      parameters?: Record<string, any>;
> 230	      /** Phase 4.2c — required for image_to_video (upstream image URL). */
> 231	      input_asset_url?: string;
> 232	      input_asset_id?: string;
> 233	      input_image_1?: string;
> 234	      input_image_2?: string;
> 235	      input_layout?: string;
> 236	      /** Phase 4.3 — cross-node consistency: upstream connected images as references. */
> 237	      reference_image_urls?: string[];
> 238	    }) => {
> 239	      const { data, error } = await supabase.functions.invoke("canvas-execute", { body: params });
> 240	      if (error) {
> 241	        let message = error.message;
> 242	        try {
> 243	          const ctx = (error as { context?: Response }).context;
> 244	          const parsed = ctx ? await ctx.json() : null;
> 245	          if (parsed?.error) message = parsed.error as string;
> 246	        } catch {
> 247	          // Fall back to original error message
> 248	        }
> 249	        throw new Error(message);
> 250	      }
> 251	      return data;
> 252	    },
> 253	    onSuccess: (_, vars) => {
> 254	      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["vm-canvas-project", vars.project_id] });
> 255	      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["vm-canvas-assets"] });
> 256	      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["vm-canvas-executions"] });
> 257	    },
> 258	  });
> 259	}
> 260	
> 261	// ── Assets & Executions ──────────────────────────────────────────────
> 262	
> 263	export function useCanvasAssets(projectId?: string) {
> 264	  const { user } = useAuth();
> 265	
> 266	  return useQuery({
> 267	    queryKey: ["vm-canvas-assets", projectId, user?.id],

> 355	      description?: string;

> 1	import type { Edge } from '@xyflow/react';
> 2	import type { CanvasNode } from '@/types/canvas';
> 3	
> 4	/**
> 5	 * Read an image URL out of a node's `data` payload. Looks at the canonical
> 6	 * output shape (`data.output.imageUrl` / `imageDataUrl`) plus the legacy
> 7	 * `data.imageUrl` field used by CharacterReference uploads.
> 8	 *
> 9	 * Centralized in Phase 4.2c so ImageToVideoNode, ImageToVideoInspector,
> 10	 * and CanvasEditorPage.handleRunAll share the exact same resolution rule.
> 11	 */
> 12	export function pickNodeImage(data: unknown): string | undefined {
> 13	  if (!data || typeof data !== 'object') return undefined;
> 14	  const d = data as Record<string, unknown>;
> 15	  const out = d.output as { imageUrl?: string; imageDataUrl?: string } | undefined;
> 16	  if (out?.imageUrl) return out.imageUrl;
> 17	  if (out?.imageDataUrl) return out.imageDataUrl;
> 18	  if (typeof d.imageUrl === 'string') return d.imageUrl;
> 19	  return undefined;
> 20	}
> 21	
> 22	/**
> 23	 * Walk the immediate upstream nodes (edges where `target === nodeId`) and
> 24	 * return the first usable image URL. One hop only — matches the existing
> 25	 * inspector/node behavior; deep BFS is not needed for the current canvas
> 26	 * topology (image-to-video has a single image source).
> 27	 */
> 28	export function findUpstreamImage(
> 29	  nodeId: string,
> 30	  nodes: CanvasNode[],
> 31	  edges: Edge[],
> 32	): string | undefined {
> 33	  const upstreamIds = edges
> 34	    .filter((e) => e.target === nodeId)
> 35	    .map((e) => e.source);
> 36	  for (const id of upstreamIds) {
> 37	    const src = nodes.find((n) => n.id === id);
> 38	    if (!src) continue;
> 39	    const url = pickNodeImage(src.data);
> 40	    if (url) return url;
> 41	  }
> 42	  return undefined;
> 43	}
> 44	
> 45	/**
> 46	 * Read ALL image URLs out of a node's `data`. For a CharacterReference mood-board node this is the
> 47	 * full `data.imageUrls` array (multiple face photos); otherwise it falls back to the single canonical
> 48	 * image (`pickNodeImage`). Used so one mood-board node contributes every photo to the reference set.
> 49	 */
> 50	export function pickNodeImages(data: unknown): string[] {
> 51	  if (!data || typeof data !== 'object') return [];
> 52	  const d = data as Record<string, unknown>;
> 53	  if (Array.isArray(d.imageUrls) && d.imageUrls.length > 0) {
> 54	    return d.imageUrls.filter((u): u is string => typeof u === 'string' && u.length > 0);
> 55	  }
> 56	  const single = pickNodeImage(data);
> 57	  return single ? [single] : [];
> 58	}
> 59	
> 60	/**
> 61	 * Collect ALL immediate upstream image URLs (edges where `target === nodeId`),
> 62	 * deduped, for cross-node consistency reference threading. Generate/style nodes
> 63	 * pass these as `reference_image_urls` so the model preserves identity from the
> 64	 * connected upstream creative(s) — including every photo of a CharacterReference
> 65	 * mood-board node. Capped at 4 (Gemini character-consistency limit).
> 66	 * SOP: docs/processes/canvas-node-consistency.md.
> 67	 */
> 68	export function findUpstreamImages(
> 69	  nodeId: string,
> 70	  nodes: CanvasNode[],
> 71	  edges: Edge[],
> 72	): string[] {
> 73	  const urls: string[] = [];
> 74	  for (const e of edges.filter((edge) => edge.target === nodeId)) {
> 75	    const src = nodes.find((n) => n.id === e.source);
> 76	    if (!src) continue;
> 77	    for (const url of pickNodeImages(src.data)) {
> 78	      if (url && !urls.includes(url)) urls.push(url);
> 79	    }
> 80	  }
> 81	  return urls.slice(0, 4);
> 82	}
> 83	
> 84	/**
> 85	 * Find the asset URL connected to a specific target handle of a node.
> 86	 */
> 87	export function findUpstreamHandleAsset(
> 88	  nodeId: string,
> 89	  targetHandle: string,
> 90	  nodes: CanvasNode[],
> 91	  edges: Edge[],
> 92	): string | undefined {
> 93	  const edge = edges.find((e) => e.target === nodeId && e.targetHandle === targetHandle);
> 94	  if (!edge) return undefined;
> 95	  const src = nodes.find((n) => n.id === edge.source);
> 96	  if (!src) return undefined;
> 97	  return pickNodeImage(src.data);
> 98	}
> 99	
> ```

> 98	  const apply = (r: CanvasRecipe) => {
> 99	    const { nodes, edges } = r.build({ x: 80, y: 80 });
> 100	    if (!nodes.length) {
> 101	      toast.error("Receita sem nós.");
> 102	      return;
> 103	    }
> 104	    if (r.pipeline) {
> 105	      setPipelineMode(true);
> 106	      setLanes(r.pipeline.lanes);
> 107	      setStyleSettings(r.pipeline.styleSettings);
> 108	      addRecipe(
> 109	        nodes,
> 110	        edges.map((e) => ({ ...e, type: "pipeline" })),
> 111	      );
> 112	      toast.success(`Pipeline "${r.name}" instalada · ${r.pipeline.lanes.length} lanes`);
> 113	    } else {
> 114	      addRecipe(nodes, edges);
> 115	      toast.success(`Receita "${r.name}" inserida · ${nodes.length} nós`);
> 116	    }
> 117	  };
> 118	
> 119	  return (
> 120	    <div className="flex flex-col gap-2 p-3">
> 121	      <div className="relative">
> 122	        <Search className="pointer-events-none absolute left-2 top-1/2 h-3.5 w-3.5 -translate-y-1/2 text-muted-foreground" />
> 123	        <Input
> 124	          placeholder="Buscar receita…"
> 125	          value={q}
> 126	          onChange={(e) => setQ(e.target.value)}
> 127	          className="h-8 pl-7 text-xs"
> 128	        />
> 129	      </div>
> 130	
> 131	      {grouped.map(([cat, items]) => {
> 132	        // While searching, force every group open so matches stay visible.
> 133	        const isOpen = q ? true : (openCats[cat] ?? false);
> 134	        const isPipeline = cat === "Pipeline";
> 135	        return (
> 136	          <div key={cat} className="overflow-hidden rounded-md border border-border bg-muted/20">
> 137	            <button
> 138	              type="button"
> 139	              onClick={() => toggle(cat)}
> 140	              className="flex w-full items-center gap-2 px-2 py-1.5 text-left hover:bg-muted"
> 141	            >
> 142	              <ChevronRight
> 143	                className={cn(
> 144	                  "h-3 w-3 text-muted-foreground transition-transform",
> 145	                  isOpen && "rotate-90",
> 146	                )}
> 147	              />
> 148	              <span className="text-[11px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-muted-foreground">
> 149	                {cat}
> 150	              </span>
> 151	              {isPipeline && (
> 152	                <span className="rounded-sm bg-accent/15 px-1.5 py-px font-mono text-[9px] uppercase tracking-wide text-accent">
> 153	                  Cinematic
> 154	                </span>
> 155	              )}
> 156	              <span className="ml-auto font-mono text-[10px] text-muted-foreground">
> 157	                {items.length}
> 158	              </span>
> 159	            </button>
> 160	            {isOpen && (
> 161	              <div className="flex flex-col gap-1.5 border-t border-border p-2">
> 162	                {items.map((r) => (
> 163	                  <button
> 164	                    key={r.id}
> 165	                    type="button"
> 166	                    onDoubleClick={() => apply(r)}
> 167	                    className="group flex gap-3 rounded-md border border-border bg-muted/40 p-2 text-left transition-all hover:border-accent/40 hover:bg-muted"
> 168	                  >
> 169	                    <RecipeThumb id={r.id} pipeline={!!r.pipeline} />
> 170	                    <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
> 171	                      <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
> 172	                        <span className="truncate text-xs font-medium">{r.name}</span>
> 173	                        {r.pipeline && (
> 174	                          <Badge variant="outline" className="h-4 gap-0.5 px-1 text-[9px]">
> 175	                            <Layers className="h-2.5 w-2.5" />
> 176	                            {r.pipeline.lanes.length}
> 177	                          </Badge>
> 178	                        )}
> 179	                      </div>
> 180	                      <div className="mt-0.5 line-clamp-2 text-[11px] text-muted-foreground">
> 181	                        {r.description}
> 182	                      </div>
> 183	                      <div className="mt-1 flex items-center gap-2 font-mono text-[10px] text-muted-foreground">
> 184	                        <span>~{r.estimatedCredits} mcoCoins</span>
> 185	                        <span>·</span>
> 186	                        <span>{r.category}</span>
> 187	                      </div>
> 188	                    </div>
> 189	                  </button>
> 190	                ))}
> 191	              </div>
> 192	            )}
> 193	          </div>
> 194	        );
> 195	      })}
> 196	
> 197	      {!grouped.length && (
> 198	        <p className="px-1 pt-2 text-center text-[11px] text-muted-foreground">
> 199	          Nenhuma receita encontrada.
> 200	        </p>
> 201	      )}
> 202	      <p className="px-1 pt-1 text-[10px] text-muted-foreground">
> 203	        Dica: duplo-clique para inserir. Receitas <span className="text-accent">Pipeline</span>{" "}
> 204	        ativam o modo cinematográfico (lanes + paleta + mood).
> 205	      </p>
> 206	    </div>
> 207	  );
> 208	}
> 209	
> 210	function RecipeThumb({ id, pipeline }: { id: string; pipeline?: boolean }) {
> 211	  if (pipeline) {
> 212	    // Mini lane × column grid preview.
> 213	    return (
> 214	      <div
> 215	        aria-hidden
> 216	        className="grid h-12 w-12 shrink-0 grid-cols-4 grid-rows-3 gap-px rounded border border-accent/40 bg-accent/5 p-0.5"
> 217	      >
> 218	        {Array.from({ length: 12 }).map((_, i) => {
> 219	          const col = i % 4;
> 220	          return (
> 221	            <span
> 222	              key={i}
> 223	              className={cn(
> 224	                "rounded-[1px]",
> 225	                col === 0 ? "bg-accent/80" : col === 3 ? "bg-violet-400/70" : "bg-foreground/30",
> 226	              )}
> 227	            />
> 228	          );
> 229	        })}
> 230	      </div>
> 231	    );
> 232	  }
> 233	  // Procedural gradient thumbnail derived from the recipe id.
> 234	  const seed = [...id].reduce((a, c) => a + c.charCodeAt(0), 0);
> 235	  const h1 = (seed * 13) % 360;
> 236	  const h2 = (seed * 47 + 90) % 360;
> 237	  return (
> 238	    <div
> 239	      aria-hidden
> 240	      className="h-12 w-12 shrink-0 rounded border border-border"
> 241	      style={{
> 242	        backgroundImage: `radial-gradient(circle at 30% 30%, hsl(${h1} 70% 55% / 0.85), transparent 60%), radial-gradient(circle at 70% 70%, hsl(${h2} 70% 50% / 0.85), transparent 60%), linear-gradient(135deg, hsl(${h1} 30% 12%), hsl(${h2} 30% 8%))`,
> 243	      }}
> 244	    />
> 245	  );
> 246	}
> 247	
> 248	/* ── Assets ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */
> 249	
> 250	import type { ImageModelId, VideoModelId } from "@/types/canvas";
> 251	
> 252	function mapModelToId(modelUsed: string | null): ImageModelId {
> 253	  if (!modelUsed) return "openrouter-gemini-image";
> 254	  const m = modelUsed.toLowerCase();
> 255	  if (m.includes("flux-1.1-pro")) return "flux-1.1-pro";
> 256	  if (m.includes("flux-schnell")) return "flux-schnell";
> 257	  if (m.includes("sdxl")) return "sdxl";
> 258	  if (m.includes("dall-e-3") || m.includes("dalle")) return "dall-e-3";
> 259	  if (m.includes("gpt-image-1")) return "gpt-image-1";
> 260	  if (m.includes("gemini-2.5-flash-image")) return "openrouter-gemini-image";
> 261	  if (m.includes("soul")) {
> 262	    if (m.includes("1080p")) {
> 263	      return "soul-1080p-x1";
> 264	    }
> 265	    return "soul-720p-x1";
> 266	  }
> 267	  return "openrouter-gemini-image";
> 268	}
> 269	
> 270	function mapVideoModelToId(modelUsed: string | null): VideoModelId {
> 271	  if (!modelUsed) return "dop-standard-5s";
> 272	  const m = modelUsed.toLowerCase();
> 273	  if (m.includes("dop-lite")) return "dop-lite-5s";
> 274	  if (m.includes("dop-turbo")) return "dop-turbo-5s";
> 275	  if (m.includes("dop-standard") || m.includes("dop")) return "dop-standard-5s";
> 276	  if (m.includes("kling")) return "kling-2.1-pro-5s";
> 277	  if (m.includes("seedance")) return "seedance-v1-pro-5s";
> 278	  return "dop-standard-5s";
> 279	}
> 280	
> 281	function AssetsTab({ projectId }: { projectId: string | undefined }) {
> 282	  const { data: assets = [], isLoading } = useCanvasAssets(projectId);
> 283	  const selectedNodeId = useCanvasStore((s) => s.selectedNodeId);
> 284	  const nodes = useCanvasStore((s) => s.nodes);
> 285	  const updateNodeData = useCanvasStore((s) => s.updateNodeData);
> 286	
> 287	  const selectedNode = nodes.find((n) => n.id === selectedNodeId);
> 288	
> 289	  const handleImportAsset = (asset: any) => {
> 290	    if (!selectedNode) {
> 291	      toast.error("Selecione um nó no canvas para importar este asset.");
> 292	      return;
> 293	    }
> 294	
> 295	    const isImageNode = ["generateImage", "sceneCompose"].includes(selectedNode.data.kind);
> 296	    const isVideoNode = selectedNode.data.kind === "imageToVideo";
> 297	
> 298	    if (asset.asset_type === "image") {
> 299	      if (!isImageNode) {
> 300	        toast.error(`Este asset é uma Imagem. Selecione um nó de Imagem para importá-lo.`);
> 301	        return;
> 302	      }
> 303	
> 304	      const params = asset.parameters ?? {};
> 305	      const modelId = mapModelToId(asset.model_used);
> 306	
> 307	      const patch: any = {
> 308	        prompt: asset.prompt ?? "",
> 309	        status: "success" as const,
> 310	        output: {
> 311	          imageUrl: asset.public_url ?? undefined,
> 312	          generatedAt: asset.created_at,
> 313	        },
> 314	        error: undefined,
> 315	      };
> 316	
> 317	      if (selectedNode.data.kind === "generateImage") {
> 318	        patch.negativePrompt = asset.negative_prompt ?? "";
> 319	        patch.modelId = modelId;
> 320	        patch.aspectRatio = params.aspect_ratio ?? params.aspectRatio ?? "9:16";
> 321	        patch.seed = params.seed != null ? Number(params.seed) : null;
> 322	        patch.fixedSeed = params.seed != null;
> 323	        patch.controls = params.controls ?? { creativity: 0.7, resemblance: 0.3, hdr: 0.5, fractality: 0.3, sharpness: 0.8 };
> 324	      } else if (selectedNode.data.kind === "sceneCompose") {
> 325	        patch.modelId = modelId;
> 326	        patch.aspectRatio = params.aspect_ratio ?? params.aspectRatio ?? "9:16";
> 327	      }
> 328	
> 329	      updateNodeData(selectedNode.id, patch);
> 330	      toast.success(`Propriedades e imagem importadas com sucesso!`);
> 331	    } else if (asset.asset_type === "video") {
> 332	      if (!isVideoNode) {
> 333	        toast.error("Este asset é um Vídeo. Selecione um nó de Vídeo (Imagem → Vídeo) para importá-lo.");
> 334	        return;
> 335	      }
> 336	
> 337	      const params = asset.parameters ?? {};
> 338	      const videoModel = mapVideoModelToId(asset.model_used);
> 339	
> 340	      const patch = {
> 341	        prompt: asset.prompt ?? "",
> 342	        videoModel,
> 343	        aspectRatio: params.aspect_ratio ?? params.aspectRatio ?? "9:16",
> 344	        motionStrength: params.motion_strength ?? params.motionStrength ?? 0.5,
> 345	        duration: params.duration ?? 5,
> 346	        status: "success" as const,
> 347	        output: {
> 348	          videoUrl: asset.public_url ?? undefined,
> 349	          generatedAt: asset.created_at,
> 350	        },
> 351	        error: undefined,
> 352	      };
> 353	
> 354	      updateNodeData(selectedNode.id, patch);
> 355	      toast.success(`Propriedades e vídeo importados com sucesso!`);
> 356	    } else {
> 357	      toast.error(`Tipo de asset não suportado para importação: ${asset.asset_type}`);
> 358	    }
> 359	  };
> 360	
> 361	  if (isLoading) return <PanelLoading />;
> 362	  if (!assets.length)
> 363	    return (
> 364	      <EmptyTab
> 365	        title="Sem assets ainda"
> 366	        subtitle="Imagens e vídeos gerados nos nós aparecem aqui para reutilizar."
> 367	      />
> 368	    );
> 369	
> 370	  return (
> 371	    <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2 p-3">
> 372	      {assets.map((a) => (
> 373	        <AssetCard
> 374	          key={a.id}
> 375	          asset={a}
> 376	          selectedNode={selectedNode}
> 377	          onImport={handleImportAsset}
> 378	        />
> 379	      ))}
> 380	    </div>
> 381	  );
> 382	}
> 383	
> 384	interface AssetCardProps {
> 385	  asset: CanvasAsset;
> 386	  selectedNode: any;
> 387	  onImport: (asset: CanvasAsset) => void;
> 388	}
> 389	
> 390	export function AssetCard({ asset, selectedNode, onImport }: AssetCardProps) {
> 391	  const isImage = asset.asset_type === "image";
> 392	  // asset.public_url is a stored URL (dead-public on a private bucket / expired-signed) — re-resolve for display.
> 393	  const displayUrl = useDisplayUrl(asset.public_url);
> 394	  return (
> 395	    <div
> 396	      onDoubleClick={() => onImport(asset)}
> 397	      className="group/asset relative overflow-hidden rounded-md border border-border bg-muted/40 transition-all duration-200 hover:border-accent/40 cursor-pointer"
> 398	      title={`Duplo clique para importar\nID: ${asset.id}${asset.node_id ? `\nNó: ${asset.node_id}` : ""}${asset.input_asset_id ? `\nPai: ${asset.input_asset_id}` : ""}`}
> 399	    >
> 400	      {/* Provenance badge on top-left of thumbnail */}
> 401	      {asset.node_id && (
> 402	        <Badge 
> 403	          variant="secondary" 
> 404	          className="absolute left-1 top-1 z-10 bg-background/80 px-1 py-0 text-[8px] font-mono hover:bg-background"
> 405	          title={`Gerado pelo nó: ${asset.node_id}`}
> 406	        >
> 407	          Nó: {asset.node_id.slice(0, 8)}
> 408	        </Badge>
> 409	      )}
> 410	      {asset.input_asset_id && (
> 411	        <Badge 
> 412	          variant="secondary" 
> 413	          className="absolute right-1 top-1 z-10 bg-accent/80 text-accent-foreground px-1 py-0 text-[8px] font-mono hover:bg-accent"
> 414	          title={`Asset pai ID: ${asset.input_asset_id}`}
> 415	        >
> 416	          Pai
> 417	        </Badge>
> 418	      )}
> 419	
> 420	      {asset.public_url && isImage ? (
> 421	        <img
> 422	          src={displayUrl ?? undefined}
> 423	          alt={asset.prompt ?? "asset"}
> 424	          loading="lazy"
> 425	          className="h-20 w-full object-cover"
> 426	        />
> 427	      ) : (
> 428	        <div className="flex h-20 items-center justify-center text-[10px] uppercase text-muted-foreground">
> 429	          {asset.asset_type}
> 430	        </div>
> 431	      )}
> 432	      <p className="truncate p-1.5 text-[10px] text-muted-foreground">
> 433	        {asset.prompt ?? asset.node_type ?? asset.asset_type}
> 434	      </p>
> 435	
> 436	      {/* Hover overlay with action button */}
> 437	      <div className="absolute inset-0 flex flex-col items-center justify-center bg-black/75 opacity-0 backdrop-blur-[2px] transition-all duration-200 group-hover/asset:opacity-100 p-2 gap-1 text-center">
> 438	        {asset.node_id && (
> 439	          <div className="text-[8px] text-muted-foreground font-mono truncate max-w-full">
> 440	            Nó: {asset.node_id}
> 441	          </div>
> 442	        )}
> 443	        {asset.input_asset_id && (
> 444	          <div className="text-[8px] text-accent font-mono truncate max-w-full" title={`Pai: ${asset.input_asset_id}`}>
> 445	            Pai: {asset.input_asset_id.slice(0, 8)}...
> 446	          </div>
> 447	        )}
> 448	        <button
> 449	          type="button"
> 450	          onClick={(e) => {
> 451	            e.stopPropagation();
> 452	            onImport(asset);
> 453	          }}
> 454	          className={cn(
> 455	            "w-full inline-flex h-7 items-center justify-center rounded text-[10px] font-semibold transition-colors mt-1",
> 456	            selectedNode
> 457	              ? "bg-accent text-accent-foreground hover:bg-accent/80"
> 458	              : "bg-secondary text-secondary-foreground opacity-60 hover:opacity-100"
> 459	          )}
> 460	        >
> 461	          Importar no nó
> 462	        </button>
> 463	        {asset.public_url && (
> 464	          <a
> 465	            href={displayUrl ?? asset.public_url}
> 466	            target="_blank"
> 467	            rel="noreferrer"
> 468	            onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
> 469	            className="text-[9px] text-cyan-300 hover:text-white underline font-mono truncate max-w-full"
> 470	          >
> 471	            Abrir original
> 472	          </a>
> 473	        )}
> 474	      </div>
> 475	    </div>
> 476	  );
> 477	}

> 484	  if (!trends.length)

> 491	  return (

> 494	        <div key={t.id} className="rounded-md border border-border bg-muted/40 p-2">
> 495	          <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-2">
> 496	            <span className="line-clamp-2 text-[11px] font-medium">{t.title}</span>
> 497	            <Badge variant="outline" className="h-4 shrink-0 px-1 text-[9px]">
> 498	              {Math.round(t.viral_score)}
> 499	            </Badge>
> 500	          </div>

> 502	            <span>{t.platform}</span>
> 503	            <span>·</span>
> 504	            <span>{t.niche}</span>
> 505	          </div>
> 506	        </div>
> 507	      ))}
> 508	    </div>
> 509	  );
> 510	}

> 528	          key={e.id}
> 529	          className="flex items-center gap-2 rounded-md border border-border bg-muted/40 p-2"
> 530	        >
> 531	          <span
> 532	            className={cn(
> 533	              "h-1.5 w-1.5 shrink-0 rounded-full",

> 535	                ? "bg-status-active"
> 536	                : e.status === "failed"
> 537	                  ? "bg-destructive"
> 538	                  : "bg-status-waiting",

> 540	          />
> 541	          <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
> 542	            <p className="truncate text-[11px]">{e.prompt ?? e.node_type}</p>
> 543	            <p className="font-mono text-[10px] text-muted-foreground">
> 544	              {e.provider}/{e.model} · {e.credits_charged} mcoCoins
> 545	            </p>
> 546	          </div>
> 547	        </div>
> 548	      ))}
> 549	    </div>
> 550	  );
> 551	}
> 552	
> 553	/* ── Shared ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */
> 554	
> 555	function PanelLoading() {

> 557	    <div className="flex h-32 items-center justify-center">
> 558	      <Loader2 className="h-4 w-4 animate-spin text-muted-foreground" />
> 559	    </div>
> 560	  );
> 561	}
> 562	
> 563	function EmptyTab({ title, subtitle }: { title: string; subtitle: string }) {
> 564	  return (
> 565	    <div className="flex h-full flex-col items-center justify-center gap-2 p-6 text-center">
> 566	      <div className="h-10 w-10 rounded-full bg-muted" />
> 567	      <div className="text-xs font-medium">{title}</div>
> 568	      <div className="text-[11px] leading-snug text-muted-foreground">{subtitle}</div>
> 569	    </div>
> 570	  );
> 571	}

> ```

> 78	
> 79	function StatusBadge({ status }: { status: GenerateImageData["status"] }) {
> 80	  if (status === "running")
> 81	    return (
> 82	      <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 rounded-full bg-accent/15 px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[9px] font-medium text-accent">
> 83	        <Loader2 className="h-2.5 w-2.5 animate-spin" /> gerando
> 84	      </span>
> 85	    );
> 86	  if (status === "success")
> 87	    return (

> 89	        <CheckCircle2 className="h-2.5 w-2.5" /> ok
> 90	      </span>
> 91	    );
> 92	  if (status === "failed")
> 93	    return (

> 95	        <AlertCircle className="h-2.5 w-2.5" /> erro
> 96	      </span>
> 97	    );
> 98	  return (
> 99	    <span className="rounded-full bg-muted px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[9px] font-medium text-muted-foreground">
> 100	      idle
> 101	    </span>
> 102	  );
> 103	}

>
> ```
> 1	import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
> 2	import { useCanvasStore } from "@/store/useCanvasStore";
> 3	import { useSaveCanvasGraph } from "@/hooks/useCanvasStudio";
> 4	
> 5	export type AutoSaveStatus = "idle" | "pending" | "saving" | "saved" | "error";
> 6	
> 7	/** Debounce window between last edit and persistence (ms). */
> 8	const AUTOSAVE_DEBOUNCE_MS = 3_000;
> 9	
> 10	/**
> 11	 * Debounced auto-save: persists the canvas graph ~1 min after the last
> 12	 * change, and flushes immediately on tab hide / unmount so a pending edit
> 13	 * is never dropped. pipelineMode / lanes / styleSettings ride inside the
> 14	 * graph jsonb blob — no separate settings write.
> 15	 */
> 16	export function useAutoSave(projectId: string | null) {
> 17	  const nodes = useCanvasStore((s) => s.nodes);
> 18	  const edges = useCanvasStore((s) => s.edges);
> 19	  const viewport = useCanvasStore((s) => s.viewport);
> 20	  const pipelineMode = useCanvasStore((s) => s.pipelineMode);
> 21	  const lanes = useCanvasStore((s) => s.lanes);
> 22	  const styleSettings = useCanvasStore((s) => s.styleSettings);
> 23	  const saveGraph = useSaveCanvasGraph();
> 24	
> 25	  const [status, setStatus] = useState<AutoSaveStatus>("idle");
> 26	  const [lastSaved, setLastSaved] = useState<Date | null>(null);
> 27	  const firstRun = useRef(true);
> 28	
> 29	  // Reassigned every render so it always closes over the latest mutation.
> 30	  const flush = useRef(async () => {});
> 31	  flush.current = async () => {
> 32	    if (!projectId) return;
> 33	    setStatus("saving");
> 34	    try {
> 35	      const state = useCanvasStore.getState();
> 36	      await saveGraph.mutateAsync({
> 37	        projectId,
> 38	        graph: state.getPersistedGraph(),
> 39	        viewport: state.viewport,
> 40	      });
> 41	      setStatus("saved");
> 42	      setLastSaved(new Date());
> 43	    } catch (e) {
> 44	      console.error("[autoSave] failed", e);
> 45	      setStatus("error");
> 46	    }
> 47	  };
> 48	
> 49	  /** Force a save immediately, bypassing the debounce. */
> 50	  const saveNow = async () => {
> 51	    await flush.current();
> 52	  };
> 53	
> 54	  useEffect(() => {
> 55	    if (!projectId) return;
> 56	    if (firstRun.current) {
> 57	      firstRun.current = false;
> 58	      return;
> 59	    }
> 60	    setStatus("pending");
> 61	    const t = setTimeout(() => {
> 62	      flush.current();
> 63	    }, AUTOSAVE_DEBOUNCE_MS);
> 64	    return () => clearTimeout(t);
> 65	  }, [projectId, nodes, edges, viewport, pipelineMode, lanes, styleSettings]);
> 66	
> 67	  // Flush on tab hide / unmount so the 1-minute window can't drop edits.
> 68	  useEffect(() => {
> 69	    if (!projectId) return;
> 70	    const onHide = () => {
> 71	      if (document.visibilityState === "hidden") flush.current();
> 72	    };
> 73	    const onPageHide = () => flush.current();
> 74	    document.addEventListener("visibilitychange", onHide);
> 75	    window.addEventListener("pagehide", onPageHide);
> 76	    return () => {
> 77	      document.removeEventListener("visibilitychange", onHide);
> 78	      window.removeEventListener("pagehide", onPageHide);
> 79	      flush.current();
> 80	    };
> 81	  }, [projectId]);
> 82	
> 83	  return { status, lastSaved, saveNow };
> 84	}
> 85	
> ```

> **Tool Result (Read)** - ✅ Success
>
> ```
> 1	import { useEffect } from 'react';
> 2	import { useQueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query';
> 3	import { supabase } from '@/integrations/supabase/client';
> 4	import { useAuth } from '@/hooks/useAuth';
> 5	
> 6	/**
> 7	 * vm_canvas_executions row shape relayed by the Realtime callback. Includes
> 8	 * the columns Phase 4.2c needs to reconcile the Zustand store on the client.
> 9	 */
> 10	export interface CanvasExecutionRow {
> 11	  id: string;
> 12	  project_id: string;
> 13	  node_id: string | null;
> 14	  status: 'pending' | 'queued' | 'running' | 'success' | 'failed' | 'cancelled' | 'timeout';
> 15	  output_url: string | null;
> 16	  error_message: string | null;
> 17	  completed_at: string | null;
> 18	}
> 19	
> 20	/**
> 21	 * Subscribes to vm_canvas_executions UPDATEs for the current user via Supabase Realtime.
> 22	 * Invalidates TanStack Query keys on every status transition AND invokes the optional
> 23	 * `onUpdate` callback so callers can reconcile the Zustand canvas store with the row.
> 24	 *
> 25	 * Used by Phase 4.2c async video flow: higgsfield-webhook flips status
> 26	 * `queued → success/failed/timeout`, this hook (a) refetches query caches and
> 27	 * (b) delegates the node-level status sync to the caller via `onUpdate`.
> 28	 *
> 29	 * Pattern mirrors `useMcorchMemory.ts:91-112` (canonical Realtime subscription).
> 30	 * RLS-aligned: `user_id=eq.<auth.uid>` filter ensures cross-tenant isolation at
> 31	 * the channel level. The callback is the single bridge between Realtime → Zustand,
> 32	 * keeping the store as the source of truth for node visual state.
> 33	 */
> 34	export function useCanvasJobsRealtime(
> 35	  projectId?: string,
> 36	  onUpdate?: (row: CanvasExecutionRow) => void,
> 37	): void {
> 38	  const qc = useQueryClient();
> 39	  const { user } = useAuth();
> 40	
> 41	  useEffect(() => {
> 42	    if (!user) return;
> 43	
> 44	    const channel = supabase
> 45	      .channel(`canvas_jobs_${user.id}`)
> 46	      .on(
> 47	        'postgres_changes',
> 48	        {
> 49	          event: 'UPDATE',
> 50	          schema: 'public',
> 51	          table: 'vm_canvas_executions',
> 52	          filter: `user_id=eq.${user.id}`,
> 53	        },
> 54	        (payload) => {
> 55	          const row = payload.new as CanvasExecutionRow;
> 56	          qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['canvas-execution', row.id] });
> 57	          if (projectId && row.project_id === projectId) {
> 58	            qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['vm-canvas-assets', projectId] });
> 59	            qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['vm-canvas-executions', projectId] });
> 60	          }
> 61	          onUpdate?.(row);
> 62	        },
> 63	      )
> 64	      .subscribe();
> 65	
> 66	    return () => {
> 67	      supabase.removeChannel(channel);
> 68	    };
> 69	  }, [user?.id, projectId, qc, onUpdate]);
> 70	}
> 71	
> ```

> 566	function SliderRow({
> 567	  label,
> 568	  hint,
> 569	  value,
> 570	  defaultValue,
> 571	  onChange,
> 572	}: {
> 573	  label: string;
> 574	  hint: string;
> 575	  value: number;
> 576	  defaultValue: number;
> 577	  onChange: (v: number) => void;
> 578	}) {
> 579	  const v = useMemo(() => value, [value]);
> 580	  return (
> 581	    <div className="mb-3">
> 582	      <div className="mb-1 flex items-center justify-between gap-2 text-[11px]">
> 583	        <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 text-muted-foreground">
> 584	          {label}
> 585	          <Tooltip>
> 586	            <TooltipTrigger asChild>
> 587	              <button type="button" className="opacity-60 hover:opacity-100" aria-label="Sobre">
> 588	                <Info className="h-3 w-3" />
> 589	              </button>
> 590	            </TooltipTrigger>
> 591	            <TooltipContent side="left" className="max-w-[16rem] text-[11px]">
> 592	              {hint}
> 593	            </TooltipContent>
> 594	          </Tooltip>
> 595	        </span>
> 596	        <button
> 597	          type="button"
> 598	          onClick={() => onChange(defaultValue)}
> 599	          className="font-mono text-foreground hover:text-accent"
> 600	          title="Reset"
> 601	        >
> 602	          {v.toFixed(2)}
> 603	        </button>
> 604	      </div>
> 605	      <Slider
> 606	        min={0}
> 607	        max={1}
> 608	        step={0.01}
> 609	        value={[v]}
> 610	        onValueChange={(arr) => onChange(arr[0] ?? 0)}
> 611	      />
> 612	    </div>
> 613	  );
> 614	}
> 615

> 617	  creativity: "Creativity",

> ```
> 1	import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";
> 2	import { Coins, Link2, Link2Off, X } from "lucide-react";
> 3	import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
> 4	import { useMemo } from "react";
> 5	import { useCanvasStore } from "@/store/useCanvasStore";
> 6	import type { CharacterReferenceData } from "@/types/canvas";
> 7	
> 8	/** Find an upstream characterReference connected via edges (1 hop). */
> 9	export function useUpstreamCharacterCandidate(
> 10	  nodeId: string,
> 11	): { id: string; data: CharacterReferenceData } | null {
> 12	  const edges = useCanvasStore((s) => s.edges);
> 13	  const nodes = useCanvasStore((s) => s.nodes);
> 14	  return useMemo(() => {
> 15	    const sourceIds = edges.filter((e) => e.target === nodeId).map((e) => e.source);
> 16	    for (const sid of sourceIds) {
> 17	      const n = nodes.find((x) => x.id === sid);
> 18	      if (n && n.data.kind === "characterReference") {
> 19	        return { id: n.id, data: n.data as CharacterReferenceData };
> 20	      }
> 21	    }
> 22	    return null;
> 23	  }, [edges, nodes, nodeId]);
> 24	}
> 25	
> 26	export function Section({
> 27	  title,
> 28	  children,

> 52	export function CostPreview({
> 53	  cost,
> 54	  balance,

> 56	}: {
> 57	  cost: number;
> 58	  balance: number;
> 59	  higgsfieldCost?: number;
> 60	}) {

> 62	  return (
> 63	    <div
> 64	      className={cn(
> 65	        "rounded-md border p-2.5",
> 66	        insufficient
> 67	          ? "border-destructive/30 bg-destructive/10"
> 68	          : "border-secondary/30 bg-secondary/10",
> 69	      )}
> 70	    >
> 71	      <div className="text-[10px] uppercase tracking-wider text-muted-foreground">

> 74	      <div className="mt-0.5 flex items-center gap-1.5 font-mono text-lg font-semibold">

> 76	          {cost} <Coins className="h-4 w-4 text-amber-400" />
> 77	        </span>
> 78	        {higgsfieldCost != null && (
> 79	          <span className="text-sm text-secondary">+ {higgsfieldCost} hf</span>
> 80	        )}
> 81	      </div>
> 82	      <div className="mt-0.5 inline-flex items-center gap-1 text-[11px] text-muted-foreground">
> 83	        Seu saldo: {balance} <Coins className="h-3 w-3 text-amber-400" />
> 84	      </div>
> 85	    </div>
> 86	  );
> 87	}

> 37	        const top = vp.y + i * LANE_HEIGHT * vp.zoom;
> 38	        const height = LANE_HEIGHT * vp.zoom;
> 39	        const isPlaceholder = lane.id.startsWith("__placeholder_");
> 40	        return (
> 41	          <div
> 42	            key={lane.id}
> 43	            className={cn(
> 44	              "absolute left-0 right-0 border-b border-dashed border-border/40",

> 70	          </div>

> 72	      })}

> 82	          onClick={() => addLane(makeLane(`Lane ${displayed.length + 1}`))}

> 90	
> 91	function LaneHeader({
> 92	  index,

> 151	          )}
> 152	          title="Renomear lane"

> 42	          <span className="h-1.5 w-1.5 rounded-full bg-status-active" /> Conectado
> 43	        </span>
> 44	      </div>
> 45	    </div>
> 46	  );
> 47	}
> 48	
> 49	function SaveIndicator({ status, lastSaved }: { status: AutoSaveStatus; lastSaved: Date | null }) {
> 50	  if (status === "pending") return <span>Auto-save pendente…</span>;
> 51	  if (status === "saving") return <span className="text-status-waiting">Salvando…</span>;
> 52	  if (status === "error")
> 53	    return (
> 54	      <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 text-destructive">
> 55	        <AlertTriangle className="h-3 w-3" /> Falha ao salvar — tentando de novo
> 56	      </span>
> 57	    );
> 58	  if (status === "saved" && lastSaved)
> 59	    return (
> 60	      <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 text-status-active">
> 61	        <CheckCircle2 className="h-3 w-3" /> Salvo {timeAgo(lastSaved)}
> 62	      </span>
> 63	    );
> 64	  return <span>—</span>;
> 65	}
> 66	
> 67	function timeAgo(d: Date) {
> 68	  const s = Math.round((Date.now() - d.getTime()) / 1000);
> 69	  if (s < 5) return "agora";
> 70	  if (s < 60) return `há ${s}s`;
> 71	  if (s < 3600) return `há ${Math.round(s / 60)}min`;
> 72	  return d.toLocaleTimeString("pt-BR", { hour: "2-digit", minute: "2-digit" });
> 73	}
> 74	
> ```

> 1	import { BaseEdge, getBezierPath, type EdgeProps } from "@xyflow/react";
> 2	import { useCanvasStore } from "@/store/useCanvasStore";
> 3	import { laneIndexFromY } from "@/types/canvas";
> 4	
> 5	/**
> 6	 * Pipeline edge. Same lane (linear "evolução") → solid accent; cross-lane
> 7	 * (desdobramento "deriva") → dashed secondary with a marching-ants animation.
> 8	 * Lane is derived from each node's Y position via laneIndexFromY.
> 9	 */
> 10	export function PipelineEdge(props: EdgeProps) {
> 11	  const {
> 12	    id,
> 13	    sourceX,
> 14	    sourceY,
> 15	    targetX,
> 16	    targetY,
> 17	    sourcePosition,
> 18	    targetPosition,
> 19	    source,
> 20	    target,
> 21	    markerEnd,
> 22	    selected,
> 23	  } = props;
> 24	
> 25	  const sourceNode = useCanvasStore((s) => s.nodes.find((n) => n.id === source));
> 26	  const targetNode = useCanvasStore((s) => s.nodes.find((n) => n.id === target));
> 27	
> 28	  const sameLane =
> 29	    sourceNode && targetNode
> 30	      ? laneIndexFromY(sourceNode.position.y) === laneIndexFromY(targetNode.position.y)
> 31	      : true;
> 32	
> 33	  const [path] = getBezierPath({
> 34	    sourceX,
> 35	    sourceY,
> 36	    sourcePosition,
> 37	    targetX,
> 38	    targetY,
> 39	    targetPosition,
> 40	  });
> 41	
> 42	  return (
> 43	    <BaseEdge
> 44	      id={id}
> 45	      path={path}
> 46	      markerEnd={markerEnd}
> 47	      style={{
> 48	        stroke: sameLane ? "hsl(var(--accent))" : "hsl(var(--secondary))",
> 49	        strokeWidth: (sameLane ? 2 : 1.5) * (selected ? 1.5 : 1),
> 50	        strokeDasharray: sameLane ? undefined : "6 4",
> 51	        opacity: selected ? 1 : 0.85,
> 52	      }}
> 53	      className={sameLane ? undefined : "pipeline-edge-dash"}
> 54	    />
> 55	  );
> 56	}
> 57	
> ```

> **Tool Result (Read)** - ✅ Success
>
> ```
> 1	import type { CanvasNode } from "@/types/canvas";
> 2	import type { Edge } from "@xyflow/react";
> 3	
> 4	/**
> 5	 * Implements Kahn's algorithm to sort Canvas nodes into topological layers.
> 6	 * Nodes within the same layer can be executed in parallel because they have no
> 7	 * dependencies on each other or their dependencies have already been satisfied
> 8	 * in previous layers.
> 9	 *
> 10	 * Throws an error if a cycle is detected.
> 11	 */
> 12	export function getTopologicalLayers(
> 13	  nodes: CanvasNode[],
> 14	  edges: Edge[]
> 15	): CanvasNode[][] {
> 16	  const nodeMap = new Map<string, CanvasNode>();
> 17	  for (const node of nodes) {
> 18	    nodeMap.set(node.id, node);
> 19	  }
> 20	
> 21	  const adj = new Map<string, Set<string>>();
> 22	  const inDegree = new Map<string, number>();
> 23	
> 24	  for (const node of nodes) {
> 25	    adj.set(node.id, new Set<string>());
> 26	    inDegree.set(node.id, 0);
> 27	  }
> 28	
> 29	  for (const edge of edges) {
> 30	    if (nodeMap.has(edge.source) && nodeMap.has(edge.target)) {
> 31	      const targets = adj.get(edge.source)!;
> 32	      if (!targets.has(edge.target)) {
> 33	        targets.add(edge.target);
> 34	        inDegree.set(edge.target, inDegree.get(edge.target)! + 1);
> 35	      }
> 36	    }
> 37	  }
> 38	
> 39	  const layers: CanvasNode[][] = [];
> 40	  let currentQueue = nodes.filter((n) => inDegree.get(n.id) === 0);
> 41	  let processedCount = 0;
> 42	
> 43	  while (currentQueue.length > 0) {
> 44	    layers.push(currentQueue);
> 45	    processedCount += currentQueue.length;
> 46	
> 47	    const nextQueue: CanvasNode[] = [];
> 48	    for (const node of currentQueue) {
> 49	      const neighbors = adj.get(node.id)!;
> 50	      for (const neighborId of neighbors) {
> 51	        const currentInDegree = inDegree.get(neighborId)! - 1;
> 52	        inDegree.set(neighborId, currentInDegree);
> 53	        if (currentInDegree === 0) {
> 54	          nextQueue.push(nodeMap.get(neighborId)!);
> 55	        }
> 56	      }
> 57	    }
> 58	    currentQueue = nextQueue;
> 59	  }
> 60	
> 61	  if (processedCount < nodes.length) {
> 62	    throw new Error("Cycle detected in Canvas Graph");
> 63	  }
> 64	
> 65	  return layers;
> 66	}
> 67	
> ```

> ```
> 1	/**
> 2	 * Shared helper to validate canvas-execute responses. The Edge Function
> 3	 * returns HTTP 200 even when the underlying provider fails (it logs the
> 4	 * error into `vm_canvas_executions.error_message` and returns a structured
> 5	 * body with `status: 'failed'` + `error: <msg>`). Inspectors must not
> 6	 * blindly treat the resolved mutation as success — they MUST inspect the
> 7	 * payload and re-throw on backend-reported failure.
> 8	 *
> 9	 * Root cause memory: smoke test 2026-05-17 — OpenRouter image endpoint
> 10	 * returned 404 HTML, backend correctly set status='failed' with
> 11	 * credits_charged=0, but inspector toast lied "Geração concluída".
> 12	 */
> 13	
> 14	export interface CanvasExecuteResult {
> 15	  execution_id?: string;
> 16	  status?: string;
> 17	  output_url?: string | null;
> 18	  asset_id?: string | null;
> 19	  credits_charged?: number;
> 20	  credits_remaining?: number;
> 21	  webhook_token?: string;
> 22	  operation_id?: string;
> 23	  error?: string;
> 24	}
> 25	
> 26	/**
> 27	 * Returns the result unchanged on success. Throws on backend-reported failure
> 28	 * so callers can use a single try/catch path. The thrown Error carries the
> 29	 * backend's pt-BR message verbatim — safe to surface via toast.
> 30	 */
> 31	export function assertExecuteSuccess<T extends CanvasExecuteResult | undefined | null>(
> 32	  result: T,
> 33	): T {
> 34	  if (!result) {
> 35	    throw new Error("canvas-execute retornou resposta vazia");
> 36	  }
> 37	  if (result.error) {
> 38	    throw new Error(result.error);
> 39	  }
> 40	  if (result.status === "failed" || result.status === "timeout" || result.status === "cancelled") {
> 41	    throw new Error(`Geração ${result.status}`);
> 42	  }
> 43	  return result;
> 44	}
> 45	
> ```
